If you don't know what that means, then you probably haven't used the pngfix script, and this comment may be ignored :) */

	background-repeat:repeat !important;
	border-color:black !important;
	border-color:currentColor !important; /* `border-color` should match font color. Modern browsers (incl. IE9) allow the use of "currentColor" to match the current font 'color' value <http://www.w3.org/TR/css3-color/#currentcolor>. For older browsers, a default of 'black' is given before this rule. Guideline to support older browsers: if you haven't already declared a border-color for an element, be sure to do so, e.g. when you first declare the border-width. */

<script>
    try {
        if (!window.localStorage || !window.sessionStorage) {
            throw new Error();
        }

        localStorage.setItem('storage_test', 1);
        localStorage.removeItem('storage_test');
    } catch(e) {
        (function () {
            var Storage = function (type) {
                var data;

                function createCookie(name, value, days) {
                    var date, expires;

                    if (days) {
                        date = new Date();
                        date.setTime(date.getTime()+(days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
                        expires = '; expires=' + date.toGMTString();
                    } else {
                        expires = '';
                    }
                    document.cookie = name + '=' + value+expires+'; path=/';
                }

                function readCookie(name) {
                    var nameEQ = name + '=',
                        ca = document.cookie.split(';'),
                        i = 0,
                        c;

                    for (i=0; i < ca.length; i++) {
                        c = ca[i];

                        while (c.charAt(0) === ' ') {
                            c = c.substring(1,c.length);
                        }

                        if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) === 0) {
                            return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
                        }
                    }

                    return null;
                }

                function setData(data) {
                    data = encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(data));
                    createCookie(type === 'session' ? getSessionName() : 'localStorage', data, 365);
                }

                function clearData() {
                    createCookie(type === 'session' ? getSessionName() : 'localStorage', '', 365);
                }

                function getData() {
                    var data = type === 'session' ? readCookie(getSessionName()) : readCookie('localStorage');

                    return data ? JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(data)) : {};
                }

                function getSessionName() {
                    if (!window.name) {
                        window.name = new Date().getTime();
                    }

                    return 'sessionStorage' + window.name;
                }

                data = getData();

                return {
                    length: 0,
                    clear: function () {
                        data = {};
                        this.length = 0;
                        clearData();
                    },

                    getItem: function (key) {
                        return data[key] === undefined ? null : data[key];
                    },

                    key: function (i) {
                        var ctr = 0,
                            k;

                        for (k in data) {
                            if (ctr.toString() === i.toString()) {
                                return k;
                            } else {
                                ctr++
                            }
                        }

                        return null;
                    },

                    removeItem: function (key) {
                        delete data[key];
                        this.length--;
                        setData(data);
                    },

                    setItem: function (key, value) {
                        data[key] = value.toString();
                        this.length++;
                        setData(data);
                    }
                };
            };

            window.localStorage.__proto__ = window.localStorage = new Storage('local');
            window.sessionStorage.__proto__ = window.sessionStorage = new Storage('session');
        })();
    }
</script>

<div class="bluefoot-row bluefoot-structural">
<div class="bluefoot-wrapper">
<hr class="bluefoot-hr bluefoot-entity" style="border-color:#ebebeb;"/><h2 class="bluefoot-entity bluefoot-entity bluefoot-align-left"><span>Angela Bruderer: Ihr Schweizer Online-Shop</span></h2><div class="bluefoot-entity lauftext bluefoot-textarea bluefoot-entity bluefoot-align-left">
<p>Angela Bruderer ist Ihr Schweizer Versandhaus für <a href="/bettwaesche">Bettwäsche</a>, <a href="/heimtextilien">Heimtextilien</a>, <a href="/damenbekleidung">Bekleidung</a>, <a href="/damen-nachtwaesche">Nachtwäsche, </a><a href="/damenunterwaesche">Unterwäsche</a> und<a href="/wohn-accessoires"> Wohn-Accessoires.</a></p></div><hr class="bluefoot-hr bluefoot-entity" style="border-color:#ebebeb;"/><div class="bluefoot-entity lauftext bluefoot-textarea bluefoot-entity bluefoot-align-left">
<p>Das Schweizer Versandhaus Angela Bruderer AG startete 1980 mit dem Versand von Strick- und Handarbeitswaren und entwickelte sich seither zum führenden Schweizer Versandhändler für qualitativ hochwertige Bettwäsche und Heimtextilien, funktionelle Bekleidung sowie Haushaltsartikel.</p>
<p>Ein tolles Einkaufserlebnis bieten Ihnen auch unsere <a href="uebersicht">Filialen</a> an den Standorten in <a href="shop-st-gallen">St. Gallen</a> und <a href="allmendingen">Allmendingen bei Bern</a>. Unsere kompetenten Verkäuferinnen beraten Sie gerne.</p></div> </div>
</div>

        var ScarabQueue = ScarabQueue || [];
        (function(id) {
            if (document.getElementById(id)) return;
            var js = document.createElement('script'); js.id = id;
            js.src = '\/' + '\/' + 'cdn.scarabresearch.com/js/13F809CE8231683F/scarab-v2.js';
            var fs = document.getElementsByTagName('script')[0];
            fs.parentNode.insertBefore(js, fs);
        })('scarab-js-api');

        
        window.Emarsys = window.Emarsys || {};
        window.Emarsys.Magento2 = window.Emarsys.Magento2 || {};

        var dataEmarsys = {"product":false,"category":false,"search":false,"useBaseCurrency":true,"slug":"","displayCurrency":"CHF"};
        var uniqueIdentifier = 'sku';

        require([
            'jquery',
            'Magento_Customer/js/customer-data'
        ], function (jQuery, customerData) {
            dataEmarsys.order = window.Emarsys.Magento2.orderData;

            let firstOnData = true;
            let timeout;

            const onData = function () {
                if (timeout) {
                    clearTimeout(timeout);
                    timeout = undefined;
                }

                // adjustment Dotsource ANB-764
                if (dataEmarsys.customer && dataEmarsys.customer.emarsysHash) {
                    //ScarabQueue.push(['setEmail', dataEmarsys.customer.email]);
                    ScarabQueue.push(['setCustomerId', dataEmarsys.customer.emarsysHash]);
                }

                if (firstOnData) {
                    var processed = 0;
                    if (dataEmarsys.product) {
                        ScarabQueue.push(['view', (uniqueIdentifier == 'product_id') ? dataEmarsys.product.id : dataEmarsys.product.sku]);
                    }

                    if (!dataEmarsys.product && dataEmarsys.category) {
                        ScarabQueue.push(['category', dataEmarsys.category.names.join(' > ')]);
                    }

                    if (dataEmarsys.search) {
                        ScarabQueue.push(['searchTerm', dataEmarsys.search.term]);
                    }

                    if (dataEmarsys.slug) {
                        if (dataEmarsys.slug) {
                            ScarabQueue.push(['availabilityZone', dataEmarsys.slug]);
                        }
                        if (dataEmarsys.slug) {
                            ScarabQueue.push(['language', dataEmarsys.slug]);
                        }
                    }

                    if (dataEmarsys.displayCurrency) {
                        ScarabQueue.push(['displayCurrency', dataEmarsys.displayCurrency]);
                    }

                    if (dataEmarsys.order) {
                        jQuery.each(dataEmarsys.order, function() {
                            if (this.email) {
                                // adjustment Dotsource ANB-764
                                //ScarabQueue.push(['setEmail', this.email]);
                                delete this.email;
                            }
                            ScarabQueue.push(['purchase', this]);
                            ScarabQueue.push(['go']);
                            processed = 1;
                        });
                    }
                }

                if (dataEmarsys.cart) {
                    ScarabQueue.push([
                        'cart',
                        dataEmarsys.cart.items.map(product => {
                            return {
                                item: (uniqueIdentifier == 'product_id') ? product.product_real_id : product.product_sku,
                                price: dataEmarsys.useBaseCurrency ? product.base_product_price_value : product.product_price_value,
                                quantity: parseInt(product.qty)
                            };
                        })
                    ]);
                }

                if (!processed) {
                    if (firstOnData) {
                                            }
                    ScarabQueue.push(['go']);
                }
                firstOnData = false;
            };

            // Adjustment dotsource ANB-764
            customerData.get('customer').subscribe(function (customer) {
                dataEmarsys.customer = customer;
                if (!timeout) timeout = setTimeout(onData, 0);
            });

            customerData.get('cart').subscribe(function (cart) {
                dataEmarsys.cart = cart;
                if (!timeout) timeout = setTimeout(onData, 0);
            });

            if (firstOnData && !(customerData.get('customer')) && !(customerData.get('cart'))) {
                onData();
            }
        });
    </script>
